# Repository Inventory

Created 2026-10-20 as the first pass of the FRD coverage review. Paths/counts establish discovery only, not completeness of behavioral analysis.

## File counts

| Artifact | Count | Root |
|---|---:|---|
| Source files | 5823 | src/ |
| App Router pages | 499 | src/app/**/page.tsx |
| API handlers | 962 | src/app/api/**/route.ts |
| UI components/hooks candidates | 1718 | src/components TS/TSX |
| Domain/service source files | 287 | src/domains, src/services |
| Validator source files | 19 | src/lib/validators |
| Test/fixture files | 1552 | tests, src/__tests__ |
| Supabase migration files | 577 | supabase/migrations |
